# API Reference

This section lists the QuantLib functions made available to Excel.


## Asianoptions

[qlContinuousAveragingAsianOption](https://github.com/frame-consulting/QuantLibXlOil/blob/main/src/quantlib_xloil/asianoptions.py#L45)(average_type: qAverageType, payoff: ql.StrikedTypePayoff, exercise: ql.Exercise, trigger = None)
